# Verification ledger — Fabric

One row per shipped REQ. `Verified by` is a person or a check that was **watched
failing** against a planted defect — a green nobody has seen go red is not evidence.
`never` in the last column is the exposure this ledger exists to count, and that count
prints beside every gate verdict so *green* never reads as *verified*.

**"suite now N probes" is HISTORICAL (M115).** Seven rows below carry a running
tally — 11, 15, 19, 22, 25, 29, 33 — and it adds up to nothing checkable: the
increments count different things in different rows, and some probes the same
ledger introduces never appear in it at all. Each number was true at its own
merge and rewriting them would falsify the record, so they stay and are labelled.
**The current count lives in `backlog.md` and is recomputed by
`node scripts/check-registers.mjs`.** No assertion count is published: the source
has 95 `ok`/`fail` call sites and a green run prints 65 lines, they measure
different things, and a number nobody can define is the thing M115 is about.

Seeded 2026-08-16 by run `2026-08-16-software-fabric`. Architecture artifacts first
shipped in commit `b68eae9`; this ledger distinguishes structural verification from
runtime verification, because no runtime is claimed by this change.

**Row ids use `<PREFIX>-REQ-<n>`.** Prefer a short prefix for new rows. The current counter in `scripts/check-registers.mjs#truth` deliberately accepts 2–6 uppercase letters and an optional word suffix after the number, retaining older BRAND/FEED rows. The former description here incorrectly said 2–3 and would undercount the existing ledger; the command is the source of the measured denominator. Table shape and prefix width are separate checks.

**Exposure: the high-level vision has 11 documented requirements shipped at `827b070`, the Fabric MCP control surface has 9 at `61101d9`, the iteration ladder 10, the slice-1 walking skeleton 7, the daily driver 7, the project workbench 8, the onboarding-and-foundations run 9, the tasks-editor-audit run 8, the agent surface 6 by `2026-08-31-agent-surface`, the memory-ADR / security-wave-1 run 12 (2 documentation, 10 security) by `2026-08-31-memory-adr-sec-wave1`, the transcripts / eval / interface-honesty run 12 by `2026-08-31-next-three`, the reconciliation / provenance / retrieval-log run 9 by `2026-09-01-reconcile-provenance`, and the bi-temporal / context-pack run 9 by `2026-09-01-bitemporal-contextpack`. **The wave-2 and wave-3 board (M42–M49) is clear. The operator's review added M50–M82; M73 and M63 ship here.** **Correction, 2026-08-31:** that sentence previously read "eight audit findings left unfixed leave with ledger ids CO-089…096 rather than as silence" — and those rows were never written; the register's next free id was still CO-089 when this run checked. The eight exist only as a count, which is the exact failure the sentence claimed to prevent. They cannot be reconstructed here without inventing them, so CO-089 now tracks recovering them by re-running the audit. **AS-REQ-005 leaves `never`: it is superseded — the bundle path it described is now covered by SEC-REQ-012 with a watched failure.** Still unverified: interactive terminal UX in the operator's hands (SCN-025 acceptance — the operator now works in `/Applications/PassionCode.app`), the down-stack autostart path under real fault injection, transcript capture (slice 2), and everything from slice 3 on.**
